- **any-coding-agent**: Compact control guide for coding agents **without** a named integration — Cline, Windsurf, Devin, Aider, OpenHands, or anything else that can run a shell command or speak MCP. The named guides ([`codex.md`](codex.md), [`claude-code.md`](claude-code.md), [`cursor.md`](cursor.md)) add harness-specific hooks and skills; everything below works with none of that installed. ([ThreeMoonsLab/agents-shipgate](https://github.com/ThreeMoonsLab/agents-shipgate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/any-coding-agent.md) · ★89)
- **any-host**: The host-neutral contract - install the guidance where your host reads it, pipe the event to magus session hook, render the verdict with -o template - for an agent host magus does not document by name. ([egladman/magus](https://github.com/egladman/magus/blob/HEAD/docs/guides/integrations/agents/any-host.md) · ★3)
- **anyrouter-integrator**: Specialist for wiring AnyRouter into an existing codebase. Use proactively when the task is "add AnyRouter", "migrate to AnyRouter", "swap LLM provider for AnyRouter", "point our agent at AnyRouter", or any equivalent. Has full context on the AnyRouter contract, model ids, routing, and MCP. ([duyet/codex-claude-plugins](https://github.com/duyet/codex-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/anyrouter/agents/anyrouter-integrator.md) · ★14)
- **anything-finder**: \--- ([Sansar35/claude-agent](https://github.com/Sansar35/claude-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/anything-finder.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **aoa-security-auditor**: AOA security auditor specializing in application security, DevSecOps, and OWASP compliance. Use when conducting security reviews, auditing dependencies, scanning for secrets, or implementing security best practices in AOA projects. ([aoalatas/aoa-ai-security](https://github.com/aoalatas/aoa-ai-security/blob/HEAD/agents/security-auditor.agent.md))
- **aof-architect**: ACD technical architect. Spawned to record design decisions as ADRs in a milestone's ARCHITECTURE.md, encode structural invariants as fitness-function arch-tests, help draw independent story boundaries, and perform STRUCTURAL code review — including codebase health (degradation is refactored within the item or ledgered in TECH_DEBT). Does not author task outcomes or implement features. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aof-architect.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-compliance**: ACD compliance specialist (the architect's conditional tier). Spawned when a change touches regulated or personal data to own the milestone's COMPLIANCE.md obligation map. Maps each obligation (GDPR, ISO 27001, …) to the control that evidences it; never edits implementation or tests. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aof-compliance.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-designer**: ACD frontend/design specialist. Spawned for milestones with UI to capture UI/UX intent in DESIGN.md, to own the "what's correct" answer for design-gap findings, and to act as a read-only fidelity judge of a screenshot it is handed (CONFORMS / GAPS / INCONCLUSIVE). Does not implement frontend code and does not run the browser. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aof-designer.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-developer**: ACD developer. Spawned (typically one per story) to implement a story's tasks — the code and the @executable step definitions that make each task's feature scenarios green, and to run the agent-runnable @manual verification (recording evidence in VERIFICATION.md). Implements against the locked contract and the ADRs; does not author outcomes or own the test-case design. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aof-developer.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-product-owner**: ACD product-owner. Spawned to author/revise a milestone's SPEC.md (objective + scope), break a milestone into independent stories, write each story's STORY.md (the user story), and triage VERIFICATION findings. Use only when work.agents.productOwner = "agent"; otherwise the main session plays this role. Does not write code or spawn other agents. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/packages/core/assets/agents/aof-product-owner.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-qa**: ACD QA. Spawned to design test cases (the Examples/case matrix for task features), perform BEHAVIOURAL (black-box) review, run the Playwright browser harness + own the toHaveScreenshot visual-regression and the optional axe-core-via-Playwright a11y lane, and broker @uat human acceptance — recording sign-off and findings in the milestone VERIFICATION.md. Does NOT do white-box/technical verification (the developer owns @manual) and does not edit production code. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aof-qa.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-researcher**: ACD researcher. Spawned to resolve a milestone's blocking unknowns and record findings in its RESEARCH.md — installed-SDK/library realities, prior-art, vendor behaviour, measured facts. Read-only on the codebase; never writes code or design decisions. ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/packages/core/assets/agents/aof-researcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **aof-security**: ACD security specialist (the architect's conditional tier). Spawned when a change has a meaningful attack surface to own the milestone's SECURITY.md threat model and write security fitness functions. Reviews against the threat model; never edits implementation (the developer fixes). ([Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework](https://github.com/Umairs-Workspace/agent-orchestration-framework/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/aof-security.md) · 3 copies)
- **aos-audit-validator**: Audits an existing or proposed AOS / multi-agent system for weak agent boundaries, hallucinated autonomy, missing validation gates, overclaiming, and audit-trail gaps. Use when the user asks "is this real autonomy?", "audit my agent system", "what's fake about this?", or before any external claim of agentic capability. ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/dotclaude/agents/aos-audit-validator.md) · ★3)
- **aos-council-director**: Orchestrates the AOS Council workflow for architecture, product, governance, risk, and execution planning. ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aos-council-director.md) · ★3)
- **aos-systems-architect**: Designs agent operating systems — governance, decision loops, memory layers, confidence/escalation policies, validation gates, and audit trails. Use when the user is designing or extending a multi-agent system, defining agent boundaries, choosing a memory architecture, or specifying how agents escalate, hand off, and prove what they did. - **aosp-juml-explorer**: AOSP (Android Open Source Project) ソースツリーに対する解析ロジックを設計する専門エージェント。「○○ を理解したい」というゴールを受け取り、どこを・どの順で・どんな仮説で見るか、Juml のどのオプションをなぜ使うかを日本語で提案する。コード実行はせず、読み取り (Read/Grep/Glob) と思考が中心。Soong (.bp) / AIDL / HAL (.hal) / sepolicy (.te) / partition の解析戦略が必要なときに委譲。 ([nigoh/Juml](https://github.com/nigoh/Juml/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aosp-juml-explorer.md))

- **aosp-log-parser**: Android log parser specialist — parses logcat, tombstone, ANR traces, and kernel logs, then merges into a unified timeline with deduplicated anomalies ([INNERJOINT/Granada](https://github.com/INNERJOINT/Granada/blob/HEAD/agents/aosp-log-parser.md) · ★1)

- **aot-graph-generator**: Use this agent to build a DAG structure of atomic questions from a complex problem. This agent creates ONLY the question graph - no solving. ## Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User asks a multi-part question requiring synthesis user: "How does React's reconciliation work and how does it compare to Vue?" assistant: "I'll use the aot-graph-generator agent to decompose this into atomic questions." &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: Planning a complex implementation user: "Help me build an authentication system with OAuth and rate limiting" assistant: "Let me build the question graph using the aot-graph-… ([snowmead/questionably-ultrathink](https://github.com/snowmead/questionably-ultrathink/blob/HEAD/claude-code/agents/aot-graph-generator.md) · ★2)
- **aot-graph-maintainer**: Use this agent to contract existing atom questions with solved answers from dependencies. This agent reads answer.md from solved atoms and rewrites question.md for dependent atoms. ## Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: A1 and A2 have been solved, need to contract A3's question assistant: "I'll use the aot-graph-maintainer agent to contract A3's question with the solved answers." &lt;/example&gt; ([snowmead/questionably-ultrathink](https://github.com/snowmead/questionably-ultrathink/blob/HEAD/claude-code/agents/aot-graph-maintainer.md) · ★2)
- **aot-judge**: Use this agent to evaluate answer quality across solved atoms at a level. This agent checks for coherence, contradictions, and completeness WITHOUT re-answering questions. ## When to use: * Rigor level is High-Stakes * After solving all atoms at a level * When additional quality assurance is needed beyond self-verification ## Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: High-stakes rigor evaluation after Level 1 solving assistant: "I'll invoke the aot-judge to evaluate answer quality before proceeding." &lt;/example&gt; ([snowmead/questionably-ultrathink](https://github.com/snowmead/questionably-ultrathink/blob/HEAD/claude-code/agents/aot-judge.md) · ★2)
